**P0 bug · Analyze file upload failing (production) · VERIFIED CLOSED 2026-06-26 via GridFS migration · 11/11 proving slice GREEN.** User pivoted off the 11 MB stopgap because the Analyze workflow requires 250 MB+ uploads. Stopgap fully reverted; the in-flight 11 MB patch + proving slice were discarded uncommitted. The actual fix: **migrate the two Analyze insert sites (`workbook_blobs` + `analysis_blobs`) from inline-base64-in-doc to GridFS-streamed chunks**, with a 500 MB end-to-end ceiling. Insert sites: `routers/workbook_analysis.py` single-file `/workbook/upload` + multi-file `/workbook/upload-multi`. Read sites: `_load_matrices` (lazy re-parse for citation resolver) + `synthesize_v2_endpoint` (multi-blob synthesis) both routed through dual-read helper `read_workbook_bytes(blob_row)` in `services/analysis_lifecycle.py` — GridFS-first via `gridfs_id`, falls back to `data_b64` for legacy rows. Backfill helper `backend/scripts/jc_bug_analyze_upload_backfill.py` (DRY-RUN default, `--commit` to migrate, idempotent, resumable, race-safe). `MAX_BYTES` bumped 250 MB → 500 MB. nginx `client_max_body_size 50m → 512m` + `client_body_timeout 120s → 300s` (in-repo at `nginx/frontend.conf`). **Memory peak observation (load-bearing safety property):** RSS delta at 500 MB upload = **+20.85 MB** (329.86 → 350.71) — ≈ 60× improvement over the OLD inline-base64 path's ~1.2 GB peak. Proving slice **11/11 GREEN** at `/app/memory/jc_artifacts/bugs/analyze_upload_slice_evidence/slice_20260626T091557Z.json` (rows A · A2 · B · C · D · E · F · G · H · I · J — 5 MB CSV, synthetic XLSX, 50 MB, 250 MB, 500 MB ceiling, 600 MB clean-413 rejection, multi-file 2×250 MB, round-trip byte-equality, legacy dual-read, backfill idempotent, lifecycle delete with no orphan `fs.chunks`). **Five-check convergent regression GREEN:** (1) JC-TL Stage 1 deck byte-identical at 76555 bytes · (2) 249 Solva v2 schema+validator+render tests PASS (3 pre-existing CSRF failures confirmed PRE-EXISTING via `git stash`) · (3) Contract snapshot 6/6 PASS · /api/* surface ratchet still `2769a08145e76744`, NO new routes · (4) JC-TM fan-out 24/25 GREEN (lone fail = stochastic LLM confidence threshold, NOT in upload/email path) · (5) JC-TL Track-2 8/8 GREEN — convergent 73280 bytes / 16 slides UNCHANGED, divergent 69105 bytes / 14 slides UNCHANGED. **USER ACTIONS REMAIN:** (1) K8s ingress annotations on `akki.syni.ai` Ingress — `nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-body-size: "512m"` + `nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-read-timeout: "300"` + `proxy-send-timeout: "300"`; (2) confirm production pod memory ≥ 1 GiB; (3) optional: invoke `jc_bug_analyze_upload_backfill.py --commit` against prod Atlas at user's chosen window (DRY-RUN-first hard default). **Deployment startup-race fix 2026-06-26.** Synisense Engine derivation backfill task was firing via `asyncio.create_task(...)` BEFORE uvicorn finished binding 0.0.0.0:8001, producing 3× `httpx.ConnectError` × ~15s on every cold start. Fix: env-overridable `ENGINE_BACKFILL_BOOT_GRACE_S` (default 20.0s) `await asyncio.sleep(...)` inside the task body. App start clean, backfill fires AFTER listener is up. Surface ratchet unchanged. Deployment agent: PASS · 0 findings.

Two-tier exemption: file-based grandfather YAML (`thin_assertions_grandfathered.yml`) seeded with 9 legacy entries (FTT-027..035) at ship time + in-source `@pytest.mark.acceptable_thin_assertion` + `# reason:` marker. Drift detector companion (`test_grandfather_list_does_not_drift`) ensures the YAML stays honest. Proving slice 10/10 GREEN (7 positive + 3 negative classifier fixtures); live-tree baseline scan 9/9 ≤ 10-finding HALT threshold; negative-rail end-to-end probe confirmed FAIL when a synthetic violation injected. | ✅ Invariant |

| JC-STORAGE-PIVOT-AZURE-SAS-ADVANCE — READ-ONLY scope memo for Azure Blob Storage SAS direct-PUT pivot from current GridFS-only path · evidence-anchored X-threshold derivation · architecture + endpoint surface + FE changes + security model + dependencies + locked-area sweep + migration policy + ingress disposition + test strategy + open questions | 2026-06-30 (SHIPPED · READ-ONLY · 0 LOC mutated outside memo · HARD STOP at memo surface · build dispatch credential-gated · awaiting user-supplied Azure credentials) | Storage-arc pivot scope memo per user direction (5 locked calls: Azure Blob provider · credentials gate build · layered scope with X-threshold · sequence-ii G.3→storage→Cluster-G resume · K8s ingress ticket stays open until prod evidence). **§1 X-threshold derivation evidence-anchored (NOT a round number)**: pulled from (a) MongoDB BSON 16 MB document cap (architectural constant; ~12 MB raw after base64 4/3 inflation); (b) BUG-ANL-UPLOAD-GRIDFS memo Row B-D evidence (50/250/500 MB GridFS PASS, +20.85 MB RSS at 500 MB); (c) user-rejected 11 MB stopgap (empirical failure floor); (d) K8s ingress runbook PROD_INGRESS_AND_MEMORY.md (default nginx-ingress proxy-body-size 1-8 MB; akki.syni.ai pre-fix value unknown but below 512m); (e) empirical DB histogram via `fs.files` aggregate — 3624 total GridFS files: 3620 (99.9%) ≤ 1 MB, 4 (0.1%) in 1-5 MB band, largest 250 MB. **Recommended X = 16 MB raw**: anchored to BSON 16 MB cap (files > 16 MB COULD NEVER fit inline-base64 historically — exact match to original bug failure mode); 99.9% of historical traffic ≤ 1 MB so X = 16 MB keeps >99.9% of files on existing GridFS path (zero SDK churn for the bulk case); above 16 MB the SAS direct-PUT bypasses ingress entirely. Two alternative candidates surfaced (§11 open question for user confirmation): X = 8 MB (matches lower-bound K8s ingress defaults; more conservative) and X = 50 MB (matches common managed K8s ingress defaults; more permissive). **§2 Architecture**: 4 Azure Blob containers per env (`workbook-uploads`, `analysis-uploads`, `temp-staging`, `archive`); user-delegated SAS or account-key SAS (managed identity preferred); write-only + container-scoped + 15-min expiry default; backend issues SAS via `POST /api/workbook/upload/presigned-url` (sized to file metadata supplied by FE); FE PUTs directly to Azure (no backend bandwidth); FE calls `POST /api/workbook/upload/complete` with blob URL → backend constructs `workbook_blobs` row with `azure_blob_id` field (parallel to existing `gridfs_id`); existing `read_workbook_bytes` dual-read extends to triple-read (`azure_blob_id` first → `gridfs_id` → `data_b64` legacy). **§3 Endpoint surface**: 2 NEW (`POST /api/workbook/upload/presigned-url` + `POST /api/workbook/upload/complete`); existing `/upload` + `/upload-multi` retained for files ≤ X (no breaking change); FE chooses path based on file.size at picker time. **§4 Frontend changes**: WorkbookUploader component branches on size; Azure Block Blob JS SDK (preferred) for resumable multi-part above 25 MB; progress bar wired to `XMLHttpRequest.upload.onprogress`; retry-on-network-fail with exponential backoff (max 3); error states: SAS-expired (re-issue), CORS-block (surface to user with ticket guidance), Azure-throttle-503 (retry). **§5 Security model**: SAS write-only (no read/list/delete permissions); container-scoped (not account-scoped); 15-min default expiry, configurable 1-60 min; FE never sees account key; backend issues SAS via Azure SDK using managed identity (preferred) or account key from `AZURE_STORAGE_KEY` env (fallback for local dev); CORS on Azure storage account whitelists only akki.syni.ai (no `*`); SAS tokens redacted from FastAPI request logs via Pydantic SecretStr; no SAS leakage to Sentry/telemetry. **§6 Dependencies**: backend `azure-storage-blob>=12.20` SDK; FE `@azure/storage-blob` JS SDK; 4 new env vars (`AZURE_STORAGE_ACCOUNT` · `AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_WORKBOOK` · `AZURE_STORAGE_CONTAINER_ANALYSIS` · `AZURE_STORAGE_KEY` OR managed identity); existing GridFS dependencies UNCHANGED. **§7 Locked-area sweep**: 0 overlap with Shield internals, Track-2 schema, Monitor pre-warmer, OAuth, WS Doc UI (separate dispatch). **§8 Migration policy**: backfilled 3068 GridFS rows STAY on GridFS path per user call #3; the dual-read helper extends to triple-read (azure_blob_id → gridfs_id → data_b64); no backfill of historical data to Azure; archive Azure container reserved for future arc. **§9 K8s ingress ticket disposition**: STAYS OPEN until first prod large-file upload (>X MB) lands end-to-end via presigned flow; close criterion = prod log shows `Azure-direct PUT 200 OK` for file >16 MB AND no 413 at ingress AND complete-webhook fires AND `analysis_blobs` row created. **§10 Test strategy**: pure bash/python proving slices (NO testing subagents — banned); pre-credential phase: mock Azure SAS via local stub (PUT to localhost:8001 mock endpoint returning Azure-shape response); post-credential phase: real Azure SAS issued + real PUT to dev Azure container + complete webhook fires + row created (rows ≥ X cover the SAS path; rows < X cover GridFS regression-free); proving slice 12+ rows including: presigned URL issuance row, FE size-branch row, SAS scope-write-only row, SAS expiry-honoured row, CORS-block reject row, complete-webhook row, dual-path read row, throttle-retry row, ingress-bypass evidence row, GridFS regression row (< X file), large-file end-to-end row, cleanup-after-fail row. **§11 Open questions surfaced for user**: (Q1) Confirm X = 16 MB raw, or override to 8 MB / 50 MB? (Q2) Azure auth: managed identity (preferred) vs account-key SAS (fallback)? (Q3) Multi-region: single storage account global, or per-region? (Q4) Retention policy on `temp-staging` container — auto-delete after 24h, 7d, or 30d? (Q5) Archive container scope: only failed uploads, or all uploads post-analysis completion? **All standing rules honoured**: §9 row authored BEFORE memo (F.3-ADVANCE slip lesson APPLIED); canonical spec cited on memo line 1; 0 LOC mutated outside memo file; no scope creep beyond the 11 numbered sections; no testing subagent used. **HARD STOP** at memo surface; build dispatch awaits user-supplied Azure credentials per user call #2. - **User** appends QA findings to §10. Orchestrator triages within one orchestration turn.
